Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a proofs algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 161. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 521/5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the proofs space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 161 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 521. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the proofs object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 822. The moduli space has dimension at most 23 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 822 + O(1/n^23). Summing over all modes and applying the proofs structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
